The state stays at 0 forever (LFSR with all-zeros is a lockup state).\n2. **test_seed**: Loads every possible seed (0x0000-0xFFFF), the sink checks show the seed value is loaded into state. The output shows a 1-cycle latency between input and state appearing.\n\nFrom the testbench:\n- Source bits format: [17:0] = {lfsr_step, lfsr_ld, lfsr_seed[15:0]}\n- Sink bits format: [16:0] = {lfsr_out_f, lfsr_state_f[15:0]}\n- On the cycle `lfsr_ld` is asserted, the seed is loaded into state. The state appears at `lfsr_state_f` on the next clock cycle.\n- On the cycle `lfsr_step` is asserted, the LFSR shifts. The new state appears at `lfsr_state_f` on the next clock cycle.\n\nLooking at the timing for test_seed:\n- Source line 1: `01_0000_0000_0000_0000` (ld=1, seed=0) \n- Sink line 1: `0_0000_0000_0000_0000` (state after reset = 0)\n- Source line 2: `01_0000_0000_0000_0001` (ld=1, seed=1)\n- Sink line 2: `0_0000_0000_0000_0000` (state=0 loaded on prev cycle)\n- Sink line 3: `0_0000_0000_0000_0000` (checking seed=0 was loaded)\n- Sink line 4: `0_0000_0000_0000_0001` (checking seed=1 was loaded)\n\nSo:\n- lfsr_state_f is a **registered output** - it reflects the current internal state (the state after the operations from the previous clock edge)\n\nThe LFSR polynomial is x^16 + x^14 + x^13 + x^11 + 1. For a standard Galois LFSR with this polynomial:\n- Bit 15 shifts out as lfsr_out_f\n- The feedback XOR taps from the output bit go into positions 14, 13, and 11\n\nWait, let me think about this more carefully. The polynomial is x^16 + x^14 + x^13 + x^11 + 1.\n\nIn a Fibonacci LFSR (standard):\n- The register shifts left\n- The new LSB (bit 0) = XOR of the tapped bits from the previous state\n- Taps for polynomial x^16 + x^14 + x^13 + x^11 + 1: bits at positions 15, 13, 12, 10 (subtracting 1 from polynomial exponents except x^16)\n\nIn a Galois LFSR:\n- The register shifts left\n- The output bit (MSB, bit 15) is XORed into the tap positions\n- Taps for polynomial x^16 + x^14 + x^13 + x^11 + 1: bits at positions 13, 12, 10\n\nLet me re-examine.
